# Recipe cards
Source: Julius “Recipes” playlist (471 videos)
Format: Remember / Do / Watch + scores
Batch size: 5

Progress: 1–174 named. Card 50 is playlist index 56 (that honey-lemon short was already in). Cards 51–62 are indices 57–68. Cards 63–74 are indices 69–80. Cards 75–174 are indices 81–181. Unavailable still 9, 11, 22, 28, 32. Set aside (not recipes): 66, 80, 89, 106, 140, 145, 146, 158, 162. Next card is 175, playlist index 182.

## 1. Vegetable Teriyaki Noodles
My Ginger Garlic Kitchen · ~20 min · serves 4  
https://youtu.be/R4SqKmbsiZY

**Remember**
- Cook noodles first. Oil them so they don’t glue.
- Veg stays crunchy. Don’t stew them.
- Sauce last. 50g bottled teriyaki is the whole sauce.

**Do**
1. Boil noodles. Drain. Rinse cold. Toss with 2 tsp oil.
2. Hot pan, 2 tbsp oil, garlic 1 min.
3. Add sliced onion, carrot, peppers, mushrooms, broccoli + pepper + chili flake. Stir until just tender.
4. 50g teriyaki. Coat. Add noodles. Toss. Salt + optional hot sauce.
5. Sesame. Peanuts only if you want them.

**Watch**
- Soft veg = cafeteria noodles.
- Salt before tasting the teriyaki and you’ll oversalt.
- Peanuts are garnish, not required.

Taste 6 · Nutrition 7 · Unique 3  
Fine weeknight bowl. Nobody talks about it the next day.

---

## 2. One-Pan Mongolian Beef Noodles
RecipeTin Eats · 15 min · serves 4  
https://youtu.be/k9X6K6nvrbY

**Remember**
- Instant noodle cakes, uncooked. Throw the seasoning packet.
- Sauce first, water second, noodles last.
- Broccoli small or it stays raw.

**Do**
Mix sauce: 2 tbsp dark soy, 3 tbsp hoisin, 2 tbsp mirin (or Shaoxing + 2 tsp sugar), ¼ tsp five-spice, 2 tsp sesame oil.

1. Hot deep pan, 2 tbsp oil. Onion + 2 garlic, 30 sec.
2. 500g beef mince. Brown. Pour in sauce. Coat.
3. 5 cups small broccoli. Stir.
4. 2¼ cups water. Simmer. Break 4 noodle cakes in half. Push in.
5. 30 sec, flip, 30 sec, then untangle. 2–3 min until sauce is gone.

**Watch**
- Boiling the noodles separately ruins the point.
- Big broccoli florets stay hard.
- Light soy works but the color goes pale.

Taste 8 · Nutrition 6 · Unique 5  
People eat the whole pan. Then they look for the recipe.

---

## 3. Brown Butter Banana Bread Cookie Bars
Well Made by Kiley · 8×8 pan  
https://youtu.be/gHDC9C0HnIg  
Full grams live on her site (recipe card is JS-gated). Method below is the part that matters.

**Remember**
- Brown the butter until amber and nutty. Cool 15–20 min.
- Exactly ½ cup (120g) mashed ripe banana. Not “a banana.”
- 8×8 pan. 9×9 makes thin sad bars.

**Do**
1. Brown salted butter. Cool.
2. Beat in brown sugar + white sugar.
3. Banana + egg + vanilla.
4. Flour + cornstarch + leavening + salt + cinnamon. Don’t overmix.
5. Fold chocolate chips / wafers. 8×8 pan.
6. Bake until edges set and center is just soft. Cool before cutting.

**Watch**
- Hot brown butter scrambles the mix.
- Extra banana = cakey, not bar.
- Cutting hot = collapse.

Taste 8 · Nutrition 3 · Unique 6  
People say “why is this better than banana bread.” Because it’s a cookie.

---

## 4. Crispy Salt & Pepper Chicken Thighs
Xiao Li’s Kitchen · ~50 min with marinade · 500g  
https://youtu.be/J_croDE-LtI

**Remember**
- Beer soak 10 min. Don’t rinse after.
- Cornstarch + oil seal before the pan.
- Skin down. Don’t touch for 3–4 min.

**Do**
1. Boneless skin-on thighs. Tap with knife back.
2. Cover with 330ml beer. 10 min. Drip off. No rinse.
3. Marinate 30 min: ginger, scallion whites, ½ onion, 1 tbsp light soy, 1 tbsp oyster, 1 tsp 椒盐 / salt-and-pepper mix.
4. Dump the aromatics. Toss chicken with 1 tbsp cornstarch + 1 tbsp oil.
5. Medium-low pan, 2 tbsp oil. Skin down. 3–4 min still. Flip. Cook through.

No 椒盐? 1 tsp salt + ½ tsp pepper + pinch five-spice.

**Watch**
- Moving the skin early tears it.
- High heat burns the coat before the meat is done.
- Rinsing after beer washes off the tenderizing.

Taste 8 · Nutrition 6 · Unique 7  
People argue about the beer. Then they make it again.

---

## 5. One-Pan Chicken Alfredo
Steel Pan Guy · ~20 min  
https://youtu.be/BatIAXoxKhk  
American fond Alfredo. Not Roman.

**Remember**
- Chicken is for the brown bits. Sauce lives on those bits.
- Cream on the fond. Cold butter + cheese off heat.
- Pinch of nutmeg in every white sauce.

**Do**
1. Preheat stainless. Water droplet should dance. Tiny bit of high-smoke oil on the chicken, not a slick in the pan.
2. Salt-and-pepper chicken breast. Sear ~2 min a side. Don’t rip it off early. Rest it out.
3. Pour cream (half-and-half or heavy) onto the fond. Scrape. Pinch nutmeg.
4. Cook fettuccine. Save a splash of pasta water.
5. Off heat: cold butter (~4 tbsp) then handfuls of Parmigiano (Romano if you have it). Toss pasta + sliced chicken. Loosen with pasta water if tight.

**Watch**
- Cheese on a raging burner = grainy sauce.
- No fond = bland cream.
- This is not authentic Alfredo. People in the comments will tell you.

Taste 8 · Nutrition 4 · Unique 4  
7.9 million views. Half the comments are “that’s not Alfredo.” The other half made it for dinner.

## 6. Fried Apple Hand Pies
Well Made by Kiley · ~2 hr 20 min · 11–13 pies
https://youtu.be/r5Tmse4kndw
https://www.wellmadebykiley.com/blog/fried-apple-hand-pies

**Remember**
- Cool the filling all the way. Warm filling leaks.
- Cold dough. Fork the seam, then fold the edge in.
- Oil 360–370°F. Use a thermometer.

**Do**
1. Filling: 58g butter, 4 diced apples, 110g brown sugar, 60g maple, cinnamon, pinch salt. ~10 min. Slurry 11g cornstarch + 1½ tbsp water, 1–2 min. Vanilla off heat. Cool.
2. Dough: 420g flour, 12g sugar, 8g baking powder (blog writes “2”), 6g salt. Cut in 170g cold butter. 7 oz cold half-and-half + 1 cold egg. Disc. Chill 30 min.
3. Roll. Cut ~4½" rounds. Heaping tablespoon filling. Egg-wash the edge. Fold, fork, fold the edge in. Chill.
4. ~2" oil. Fry ~2 min a side. Glaze warm: 120g powdered sugar, 58g browned butter, 80g maple, 1 tbsp milk, vanilla, pinch salt.
5. Oven instead: 400°F, 22–25 min, egg wash, a vent slit.

**Watch**
- More than a heaping tablespoon will not seal.
- Kneading makes a tough crust.
- Once frying, hold about 350–365°F. Low = greasy. High = brown outside, raw inside.

Taste 8 · Nutrition 2 · Unique 5
79k views. People call them a favorite. Fried, not baked, is the reason.

No peanuts. No legumes.

## 7. Air Fryer Vietnamese Lemongrass Chicken Noodle Bowl
Karen Law · under 30 min · serves 2
https://youtu.be/oU4Hv2L-9r8
https://daigasikfaan.co/air-fryer-vietnamese-lemongrass-chicken/

**Remember**
- Skin-on thighs. Breast dries in the air fryer.
- Rinse the noodles cold so they stay bouncy.
- Taste the nuoc cham before it hits the bowl.

**Do**
1. Marinade on 4 deboned skin-on thighs: 1 minced lemongrass, 3 tbsp fish sauce, 2 tbsp sugar, 1 tbsp light soy, 1 tbsp garlic, ½ tbsp cornstarch, juice of ½ lime.
2. Nuoc cham: 4 tbsp fish sauce, 2 tbsp brown sugar, 1 tbsp garlic, 1 bird’s eye chili, juice of 1 lime, ⅓ cup warm water.
3. Rice vermicelli per the package, then rinse cold. Amount not listed. One nest a person.
4. Air fry skin down, 185°C (365°F), 13–15 min. Flip halfway.
5. Slice over noodles with julienned cucumber and carrot, gem lettuce, coriander. Drizzle sauce.

**Watch**
- Breast, same time, goes dry.
- Skip the rinse and the noodles glue.
- Don’t store it mixed. Chicken, noodles, and veg apart, up to 3 days. Fresh sauce when you eat.

Taste 8 · Nutrition 7 · Unique 6
47k views. Quiet comments. A weeknight bowl people finish.

No peanuts. No legumes.

## 8. Vanilla Burnt Basque Cheesecake
Notorious Foodie · time unknown · 8 slices, 9.5" tin
https://youtu.be/_t8spHjFW9M
Paid recipe: https://notoriousfoodie.substack.com/p/burnt-basque-cheesecake-a-recipe

Thin card. Description empty. No captions. Weights are behind the paywall. Do not use his other Basque post. That one is an 8" tin and a different recipe.

**Remember**
- No biscuit base. High heat. The top should go dark.
- Centre is still jiggly when it comes out.
- It sinks in the middle. Rest it before you cut.

**Do**
1. Room-temp ingredients so it blends smooth.
2. Parchment up the sides so it can rise and look rough.
3. Fresh vanilla beans if you have them. Amount not in the free text.
4. Bake hot until the top is charred, not tan. Temp and time: unknown.
5. Cool until it settles, then slice.

**Watch**
- Pale top plus a long bake kills the gooey middle.
- Cutting hot and it slumps.
- No parchment and it sticks and can’t rise.

Taste 8 · Nutrition 3 · Unique 7
About 2 million views on the Short. People want the dark top. The grams live on the paid post.

No peanuts in the free text. Paid list not seen. No legumes in the free text.

## 9. Smoked Chuck Roast
jackieh · smoke 3 hr + crockpot 3–4 hr · 2–3 lb
https://youtu.be/1GNM2FCi02E
Recipe is the video description. No blog.

**Remember**
- Smoke at 225°F for 3 hours. Flip once.
- Then broth halfway up the meat. Crockpot on high, 3–4 hours. Flip once.
- Store it in some of the liquid or it dries.

**Do**
1. Coat a 2–3 lb boneless chuck with oil and a steak rub. He uses Black & Tan by Fire & Smoke.
2. Smoke 225°F, 3 hours, flip halfway.
3. Crockpot on high. Add 1–2 cups bone broth or beef stock, about halfway up.
4. 3–4 hours, flip halfway.
5. Cut big chunks, then split with a fork. Don’t shred the whole roast into strings.

**Watch**
- A full shred goes stringy.
- A dry container tomorrow means you left the liquid behind.
- Too little broth and it never half-covers.

Taste 8 · Nutrition 6 · Unique 4
2.8 million views. The method is the whole recipe. People make it because the steps are short.

No peanuts. No legumes.

## 10. Vietnamese Chicken Noodle Soup (Phở Gà)
Julia Vuong · 20 min pressure + 10 min release · serves 4
https://youtu.be/4J4Pcd7mcX8
YouTube description empty. Card from her Instant Pot post: https://instantpot.com/blogs/recipes/vietnamese-chicken-noodle-soup

**Remember**
- Bone-in chicken. That’s the broth.
- Char the onion and ginger. Toast the spices.
- Go light on fish sauce. It darkens the soup. More bouillon if you want savor.

**Do**
1. Char 1 large onion, quartered, and sliced ginger. Toast 5 star anise, 5 cloves, 2 bay leaves, 1 cinnamon stick, 5 peppercorns.
2. Instant Pot: those, a whole chicken, 8 cups water, 1½ tbsp chicken bouillon, 1½ tbsp rock sugar. High pressure 20 min. Natural release 10. Skim.
3. 2 tbsp fish sauce, tasted, not dumped.
4. Sauce: 3 tbsp neutral oil, minced ginger and 2 tbsp scallion, 30 seconds. ½ tsp garlic salt. 1 tsp sesame oil.
5. 24 oz rice noodles, cooked to the package. Bowl with broth, chicken, sliced onion, cilantro, lime, and the sauce.

**Watch**
- Too much fish sauce turns the broth dark.
- Skip the char and the toast and it tastes like plain chicken water.
- Leave the scum and the bowl looks gray.

Taste 8 · Nutrition 7 · Unique 6
399k views. Fast phở, not a six-hour pot. The bones still did the work.

No peanuts. No legumes.

## 12. Egg Drop Soup
Jujumao · stock 1–2 hr · serves unknown
https://youtu.be/tZoscy6Fc0M

**Remember**
- Blanch the chicken and the feet. Throw that water out.
- Gentle simmer, then strain.
- Flick the eggs forward. Do not dump them.

**Do**
1. Blanch 1 kg chicken feet and 1 whole chicken with a thumb of sliced ginger and 2 tbsp Shaoxing, 5–10 min. Discard the liquid. Rinse the scum.
2. Soak 3/4 cup dried mushrooms, and 1/2 cup dried scallops if you want them, 15–30 min.
3. Clean pot: chicken, 3 smashed ginger pieces, 1 cup chopped green onion, 1 medium radish, the mushroom liquid, 1–2 tbsp Shaoxing, water to cover. Simmer 1–2 hr. Strain.
4. Season: fish sauce, 2 tsp white pepper, 1 tsp sugar, 2 tbsp sesame oil, 1/4 cup light soy, salt. Thicken with potato starch. Start at 1/2 cup starch in a little water.
5. Stream in 6 beaten eggs, flicking. Sliced scallions.

**Watch**
- Skip the blanch and the stock stays cloudy.
- Too much slurry and it turns glue.
- Eggs dumped in become clumps, not ribbons.

**Tools** (hide)
- Pot for the blanch, pot for the simmer, strainer, bowl for the eggs and the slurry.

Taste 8 · Nutrition 6 · Unique 7
6.9 million views. People put it on when they feel sick. The stock is the point.

Legume: light soy sauce. No peanuts. No tofu, beans, peas, or lentils. Dried scallops are optional.

## 14. Fried Cherry Hand Pies
Well Made by Kiley · ~2 hr 5 min · 11–13 pies
https://youtu.be/d-AnxjwbiFk

Same dough family as her apple hand pies.

**Remember**
- Filling thick and cold before it goes in.
- Cold dough. Do not knead it.
- Oil at 360–370°F.

**Do**
1. Filling: 530g cherries, 100g sugar, juice of 1/2 lemon, pinch of salt, 6–8 min. Slurry 12g cornstarch plus 1½ tbsp water, 1–2 min. 1 tsp vanilla. Chill.
2. Dough: 420g flour, 12g sugar, 1 tsp baking powder, 6g salt, 170g cold salted butter, 7 oz cold half-and-half, 1 cold egg. Chill 30 min.
3. Cut 4½" rounds. Heaping tablespoon. Egg-wash the edge. Fork. Fold the edge in.
4. Fry about 2 min a side. Glaze: 120g powdered sugar, 2 tbsp milk, 1 tsp vanilla, pinch of salt.
5. Oven instead: 400°F, 22–25 min, egg wash, a vent slit.

**Watch**
- Overfill and it leaks.
- A weak fork seal leaks.
- Oil too cool is greasy. Too hot browns before the middle cooks.

**Tools** (hide)
- Saucepan, rolling pin, 4½" cutter, fork, heavy pot, oil thermometer, slotted spoon, rack.

Taste 8 · Nutrition 2 · Unique 5
108k views. Same crust as the apple pies. Cherries are the swap.

No peanuts. No legumes.

## 15. Italian Stromboli
Carman Wilken · ~30 min · serves 8
https://youtu.be/ziwKFRa3plc

**Remember**
- Leave a border. Pinch every seam. Seam side down.
- Cut slits so steam can leave.
- Rest 5 min or the cheese runs out.

**Do**
1. One refrigerated crust, about 13 oz. Spread 1/4 cup pizza sauce, not the whole jar.
2. Layer 1/4 lb ham, 1/4 lb salami, 1/4 lb pepperoni, 3 cups grated mozzarella, 1 tsp Italian seasoning.
3. Roll, pinch, seam down. Slits on top.
4. Brush 2 tbsp melted butter, garlic powder, dried parsley. The last two have no amount.
5. 400°F, about 20 min. Rest 5. Rest of the sauce for dipping.

**Watch**
- Open seams and the filling escapes.
- Cut it hot and it is a puddle.
- Stretch the dough thin and it tears.

**Tools** (hide)
- Baking sheet, parchment, pastry brush, knife, oven.

Taste 7 · Nutrition 4 · Unique 3
3.7 million views. The deli counter did the hard part.

No peanuts. No legumes.

## 16. Cheesy Baked Ziti
Allrecipes · Nicole’s Best Baked Ziti · 1 hr 25 min + 10 min rest · serves 8
https://youtu.be/uV92qpSSY9Y

**Remember**
- Boil the pasta 1–2 min short of the package.
- Ricotta is its own layer. Do not stir it through.
- Stand 10 min before you scoop.

**Do**
1. Oven 375°F. Grease a 9×13. Boil 16 oz ziti with 1 tbsp kosher salt, about 8 min.
2. Brown ¾ lb Italian sausage and ¾ lb ground sirloin. Add 1 small onion, 2 garlic cloves, salt, pepper, ¼ tsp chili flake, 3–5 min.
3. Two 24 oz jars marinara, plus ¼ cup water shaken in each jar. Simmer 10 min. Toss the pasta with a little sauce.
4. Ricotta mix: 15 oz whole-milk ricotta, ½ cup Parmesan, 1 egg, 1 tbsp parsley, 1 tbsp basil, remaining salt and pepper.
5. Layer sauce, pasta, sauce, ricotta, mozzarella, the rest of the pasta and sauce and cheese, last ¼ cup Parmesan. Bake 25–30 min. Rest 10.

**Watch**
- Pasta cooked all the way on the stove goes mushy in the oven.
- Skip the jar rinse and the sauce comes up short.
- Serve it straight from the oven and the layers fall apart.

**Tools** (hide)
- 9×13 dish, large pot, deep skillet, mixing bowl, oven.

Taste 8 · Nutrition 5 · Unique 3
292k views. A tray people already know. The layering is why this version.

No peanuts. No legumes.

## 18. Burnt Basque Cheesecake
Mai Cookbook · 30 min bake + at least 2 hr rest · serves 6
https://youtu.be/jB32_Fy7l1Y

Use her written recipe. She says the old video amounts differ.

**Remember**
- 6×3 inch tin. High heat. The top should darken.
- Leave it in the tin at least 2 hours. A hot unmold collapses it.
- Updated weights only.

**Do**
1. Heat the oven to 240°C. Crumpled parchment in a 6×3" tin.
2. 400g cream cheese and 120g sugar, smooth. 3 eggs, one at a time. ½ tsp vanilla and 200ml heavy cream. Sift in 20g cornstarch.
3. Pour. Tap out bubbles. Bake 30 min at 220–240°C, lower rack.
4. Rest in the tin at least 2 hours. Fridge overnight if you want it firmer.

**Watch**
- The video’s old quantities do not match the post.
- Unmold it hot and it slumps.
- Too dark: drop toward 220°C. More cornstarch or 5 more minutes if you want it firmer.

**Tools** (hide)
- 6×3" tin, parchment, bowl, sieve, oven.

Taste 8 · Nutrition 3 · Unique 6
2.9 million views. Dark top, soft middle. This one has the grams. The earlier Notorious Foodie short does not.

No peanuts. No legumes.

## 20. Caramel Apple Pie Ice Cream
Sara J Sweets · Ninja Creami · serves unknown
https://youtu.be/IDDMCFMpugE

Ingredients are in the description. Freeze time and which Creami cycle are not.

**Remember**
- Fairlife is the protein trick. Regular milk works and drops the protein.
- Caramel and spice go in the base. Apple is a mix-in.
- Do not invent a spin setting. She did not write one.

**Do**
1. Base: 2½ cups Fairlife or regular milk, 3 tbsp Torani zero-calorie caramel, ¼ tsp cinnamon, ⅛ tsp nutmeg.
2. Mix-in: ¼ cup chopped apple, 1 tbsp maple syrup, ½ tsp vanilla, ½ tsp cinnamon, ¼ tsp nutmeg, 1 tbsp heavy cream.
3. Freeze in a Creami pint. Button and time: unknown.

**Watch**
- Swapping the milk and expecting the same protein.
- Stirring the apple mix into the base. It is separate.
- Guessing a respin. Not in the text.

**Tools** (hide)
- Ninja Creami, pint container, knife for the apple.

Taste 6 · Nutrition 6 · Unique 5
56k views. A pint, not a churn. The machine does the work.

No peanuts. No legumes.

## 21. Mongolian Chicken
iamneverfull · 30 min · serves 2
https://youtu.be/k88KnzlDP1Q
https://iamneverfull.com/mongolian-chicken/

**Remember**
- Sear the chicken so the sauce has something to grab.
- Dashi is the restaurant taste. Bouillon works if you have no dashi.
- Char the onion and scallion whites before the sauce goes in.

**Do**
1. 16 oz boneless skinless thighs, bite size. 1 tbsp Shaoxing, ½ tsp salt, 1 tbsp cornstarch. 15 min.
2. Sauce: 1 tbsp dark soy, 1 tbsp light soy, 1 tsp dashi, 3 tbsp water, 1½ tbsp sugar.
3. Medium-high oil. Sear chicken about 5 min. Out.
4. Same pan: 1 onion in chunks and scallion whites, 2–3 min, a little char.
5. Chicken back in with the sauce until it coats. Scallion greens at the end. Rice if you want it.

**Watch**
- No dashi and it tastes flatter. Use bouillon or skip it, but know the difference.
- A crowded pan will not brown.
- Peanuts or cashews are a tip, not the dish. Skip the peanuts.

**Tools** (hide)
- Frying pan, two bowls.

Taste 8 · Nutrition 6 · Unique 5
1.4 million views. People want the takeout chicken without the batter.

Legume: dark soy and light soy. Peanuts optional. Skip them.

## 22. Beef Pot Roast with Gravy
Sara · Nutrient Matters · about 3 hr 40 min · servings unknown
https://youtu.be/2P3amMdDNqE
https://nutrient-matters.com/recipe/beef-pot-roast-with-gravy/

**Remember**
- Sear it. The gravy lives on that fond.
- 325°F, covered, about 3 hours.
- Strain the liquid and press the onion and garlic before you make gravy.

**Do**
1. Oven 325°F. 2 lb bottom blade roast. Montreal seasoning, salt, pepper.
2. Dutch oven, 2 tbsp beef tallow. Sear 5–7 min a side. Out.
3. Spanish onion 4–6 min. 4–6 garlic cloves 30–60 sec. 1 tbsp tomato paste 2–3 min.
4. Whisk 1 tbsp brown sugar, 1 tbsp soy, 1 tbsp ketchup, 1 tbsp apple cider vinegar. Deglaze.
5. Beef back in. 2 cups beef broth, 2 cups chicken broth, rosemary, thyme, 2 bay leaves. Cover. About 3 hours. Carrots in for the last 45–60 min.
6. Strain. Roux: 4 tbsp butter and 4 tbsp flour, 2–3 min, then 4 cups of that liquid. ½ tsp white pepper and a little browning sauce. Half the liquid means half the roux.

**Watch**
- Burn the fond and the gravy is bitter.
- Wrong roux ratio and it is paste or water.
- Carrots in at the start go soft. Later they keep a bite.

**Tools** (hide)
- Dutch oven, sieve, small saucepan. Instant Pot and slow cooker are blog variants, not this card.

Taste 8 · Nutrition 6 · Unique 4
4.9 million views. Sunday meat. The gravy is why they replay it.

Legume: soy sauce. No peanuts. No beans or peas.

## 23. Wet Burrito
Zhane Williams · bake 20 min · servings unknown
https://youtu.be/PMLF4cm8WSI

Partial. Description plus the spoken steps. Cheese amounts and tortilla count are not given.

**Remember**
- Drain the fat, then add water so the seasoning doesn’t clump.
- Refried beans go in the beef, not on the side.
- 375°F until the cheese is bubbling, about 20 min.

**Do**
1. Brown 1 lb ground beef with 2 packs taco seasoning. Drain the fat. Add water. Amount of water unknown.
2. Stir in 1 can refried beans.
3. Large flour tortillas. Beef, Mexican-blend cheese, mozzarella. Roll. Counts unknown.
4. More cheese, 2 cans enchilada sauce, more cheese.

## 24. Mexican Beef Stew (Guisado de Res)
Love Kari Cooking · about 2 hr · servings unknown
https://youtu.be/jU5g7tPiXto

**Remember**
- Flour on all sides, then brown in batches.

**Do**

2. Oil, medium. Brown in batches, about 4 min a batch. Don’t crowd. Oil amount unknown.
3. Same pan: ½ white onion and 3 garlic, about 1 min. ½ red pepper and 1 Roma tomato until it gives juice.
4. 2 tbsp tomato paste, 4 cups beef broth, 2 tsp bouillon, ½ tsp oregano crushed in your hand. Boil.
5. Meat back in, 2 bay leaves. Cover, low, 1½ hours.
6. 2 potatoes and 1 carrot. Cover 20 min more. Rice and cilantro if you want them.

**Watch**
- One crowded batch and the meat steams.
- Potatoes in early and they dissolve.
- Oil amount is not written. Use enough to brown, not to fry.

## 26. Shepherd’s Pie
Sara · Nutrient Matters · 55 min · 11×8 dish
https://youtu.be/6aY90Olkd5I
https://nutrient-matters.com/recipe/shepherds-pie/

**Remember**
- Cook the flour in the beef fat. That is the thickener.
- Egg yolks in the mash so it bakes creamy and still holds.
- Fork ridges and extra Pecorino. That is the crust.

**Do**
1. Brown 2 lb minced beef with salt and pepper. Yellow onion and 1½ cups shredded carrot, 2–3 min. 3–4 garlic cloves, 60 sec.
2. 2 tbsp tomato paste. 3 tbsp flour, 2–3 min. Add 1–2 tbsp oil if the beef was lean.
3. 1½ tbsp soy, 1 heaping tbsp ketchup, 1½ tbsp white vinegar. 2½ cups broth (or water plus bouillon: 1 beef cube and ½ chicken). Rosemary, ½ tsp thyme, ½ tsp allspice. Cover, simmer about 10 min. 1 cup frozen peas for the last 5.
4. Oven 400°F. Boil 2½–3 lb yellow potatoes 10–15 min. Dry them in the pot. Mash with 1 stick butter, ½ cup warm heavy cream, ¾ cup Pecorino, 2 tsp parsley, ½ tsp garlic powder, 2 egg yolks. One line on the blog says milk. Use the cream.
5. Beef in an 11×8 dish. Mash on top. Fork plus a little olive oil. Pepper and more Pecorino.
6. 20–25 min. Broil 2–3 min. Rest 5.

**Watch**
- Spreading the mash hard stirs up the meat. Pipe it if you can.
- Lean beef and the flour burns. Add the oil.
- Watery mince needs longer than 10 min. The blog’s readers hit this.

**Tools** (hide)
- Deep pan, pot, masher or ricer, 11×8 baking dish, fork. Piping bag optional.

Taste 8 · Nutrition 5 · Unique 4
3.8 million views. Winter pie. People come back for the crust, not the mince.

Legumes: soy sauce, and frozen peas. Both required. No peanuts.

5. Chicken back in until cooked through.

## 37. Lemon Brûlée
Ed Cudlipp · chill 6 hr · 6 shells from 3 lemons
https://youtu.be/bKViPvxoHe0

**Remember**
- Unwaxed lemons. You eat the shell’s neighbor, the zest.
- Simmer cream and sugar until it coats a spoon.
- Chill at least 6 hours. Torch the brown sugar until it is hard.

**Do**
1. Halve 3 lemons. Sieve the flesh, about 110 ml.
2. 500 ml whipping cream, 120 g white sugar, zest of a fourth lemon. Simmer until it coats a spoon.
3. Fill the shells. Chill 6 hours or overnight.
4. Brown sugar on top. Amount not listed. Torch until hard.

**Watch**
- Waxed lemons and the zest tastes like wax.
- Pull the cream early and it will not set.
- Serve before the sugar sheet is hard.

**Tools** (hide)
- Sieve, saucepan, fridge, kitchen torch.

Taste 8 · Nutrition 3 · Unique 8
1.8 million views. Posset in the lemon it came from.

No peanuts. No legumes.

## 46. Vietnamese Lemongrass Chicken
Genio Ng · 25 min plus at least 1 hr marinade · serves 3
https://youtu.be/ivcjY_u3LJc
https://cookingwithgenius.com/vietnamese-lemongrass-chicken/

**Remember**
- Fresh lemongrass, the white part, minced fine.
- Overnight in the fridge if you can. One hour is the minimum.
- Don’t crowd the pan. You want char, not steam. Rest 2–3 min before you slice.

**Do**
1. 6 boneless thighs, about 750–900 g.
2. Marinade: 2 tbsp minced lemongrass, 2 tbsp shallot, 6 garlic, 1½ tsp ginger, 3 tbsp oyster sauce, 1½ tbsp soy, 1 tbsp fish sauce, 1½ tbsp honey, 1½ tbsp brown sugar, 1½ tbsp oil. At least 1 hour, overnight better.
3. Nuoc cham: ¼ cup warm water, 2½ tbsp sugar, 2 tbsp fish sauce, 2 tbsp lime, 3 garlic, 2 bird’s eye chilies.
4. Medium skillet or grill pan, 5–6 min a side. Leftover marinade in for the last minute.
5. Rice, pickles, cucumber, the sauce.

**Watch**
- No marinade and it is just grilled chicken.
- Dried lemongrass will not smell like this.
- Slice it hot and the juice runs out.

**Tools** (hide)
- Bowl, skillet or grill pan.

Taste 8 · Nutrition 6 · Unique 5
1.5 million views. A second lemongrass chicken on the list. This one is pan, not air fryer, and it has soy.

Legume: soy sauce. Oyster sauce often has soy. Peanuts are not in this version. A wrap-bar note on the blog mentions them as optional. Skip them.

## 47. Beef Bourguignon
Pinch of Mint · 1½–3 hr · serves 4–6
https://youtu.be/dQPO7VAXgAY
https://www.pinchofmint.com/post/classic-beef-bourguignon-red-wine-braised-beef-stew

**Remember**
- Sear the beef in batches. Deep brown.
- Tomato paste, then flour, a minute or two each.
- Mushrooms and shallots are browned on their own, then 20 min in the stew.

**Do**
1. 1 kg chuck or brisket, 4–5 cm cubes. Salt and pepper.
2. 30 ml olive oil. Crisp 300 g bacon lardons. Sear the beef in batches with 20 g butter.
3. 2 onions, 4 carrots, 2 garlic. 40–45 g tomato paste, 1–2 min. 30 g flour, 1–2 min.
4. Beef and bacon back in. 750 ml red wine, 300 ml beef stock, thyme, 2 bay leaves. Stovetop 1½–2 hr, or 160°C fan for 2–3 hr.
5. Brown 200 g button mushrooms. Caramelize 6 halved shallots cut side down, with oil and 20 g butter. Into the stew 20 min. Parsley.
6. Mash, bread, or buttered pasta.

**Watch**
- The sauce gets too thick. A splash of water.
- Crowded beef steams.
- Mushrooms dumped in raw and they stay pale.

**Tools** (hide)
- Dutch oven, frying pan, lid.

Taste 9 · Nutrition 5 · Unique 4
4 million views. The short version of a long stew. People stay for the separate shallots.

No peanuts. No legumes.

## 49. Caramelized Onion Chicken Pasta
Jalalsamfit · about 1 hr · serves 6
https://youtu.be/vAzMBfiVDaY

His macros: about 638 calories and 58 g protein a serving. Use them as his label, not a lab test.

**Remember**

- Dairy goes into the dish while it is still hot. Stir the whole time.
- Chicken gets a broil at the end so it crisps.

**Do**
1. 1200 g boneless thighs, cubed. 2 tsp salt, 4 tsp spicy Italian seasoning, 3 tsp parsley, 4 tsp garlic powder, 4 tsp paprika, 6 tsp olive oil. Set aside.
2. 200 g red onion and 200 g white onion, sliced. 100 g chopped sun-dried tomato. 1 tsp salt, 3 tsp Italian seasoning, 2 tsp paprika. A spray of avocado oil. A whole garlic bulb, top cut, in the middle. Foil. 45 min at 190°C / 380°F.
3. Squeeze the garlic. While hot: parsley, 20–25 g dark soy, 90–100 g Parmesan, 400–450 g evaporated milk or milk, 120 g light cream cheese. Stir smooth.
4. Fold in 350 g cooked spaghetti or linguine.
5. Chicken on a sheet, 18 min at 200°C / 400°F, then broil 5 min. Serve on the pasta.

**Watch**
- Cold dish plus cream cheese and it stays lumpy.
- No foil, or no halfway look, and the onions dry out.
- Skip the broil and the chicken stays pale.

**Tools** (hide)

Taste 8 · Nutrition 7 · Unique 6

## 56. Medieval Bokenade
Tasting History with Max Miller · a few hours · servings unknown
https://youtu.be/jq-D_73TfLI
https://www.tastinghistory.com/recipes/bokenade

The short was blocked. Amounts are from his Bokenade page, the dish this tavern clip belongs to.

**Remember**
- Meat on the bone, covered, about an hour. Then strain the broth.
- Herbs and spices simmer another 1–2 hours. Top up the liquid.
- Egg yolks and verjuice go in off the heat, at the end. Don’t boil it again.

**Do**
1. 3–4 lb beef, goat, or chicken, with bones. Water or broth to cover. Boil, then simmer about 1 hour. Skim. Pull the meat, cut it, strain the broth.
2. Meat and broth back in. A small handful of parsley, a few sage leaves, a few sprigs of hyssop or about 1 tsp dried. ½ tsp mace, ⅛ tsp cloves. Covered, 1–2 hours.
3. Off the heat. Cool a little. Temper 4 egg yolks with about ½ cup hot broth. Whisk in ½ tsp ginger, a pinch of saffron, 1 tsp salt.
4. Stir that in with ½ cup verjuice. If you have no verjuice: equal parts grape juice and red wine vinegar.

**Watch**
- A hard reheat after the yolks and they scramble.
- Letting the second simmer run dry.
- Skipping the verjuice. The sour is the point.

**Tools** (hide)
- Large pot, strainer, whisk.

Taste 6 · Nutrition 5 · Unique 9
A medieval tavern stew. Thickened with yolks, not a roux.

No peanuts. No legumes.

5. Bowl: fries, chopped beef, 70 g sauce, leftover onion, 100 g tomato, 100 g red onion, 100 g dill pickle, sesame seeds.

https://youtu.be/9dqlqEyBKro

Partial. Amounts are from on-screen text. The video is in Spanish. No oven.

**Remember**
- Caramel first, medium-low: butter and sugar, then the bananas.
- The batter cooks covered, very low, 15–20 minutes.
- Flip. Five more minutes.

**Do**
1. 20 g butter and 20 g sugar. Caramelize. 2 bananas or plantains.
2. Batter: 1 egg, 50 g sugar, 8 g vanilla sugar, 50 ml oil, 100 ml milk, 130 g flour, 20 g cocoa, 8 g baking powder.
3. Cover. Very low, 15–20 min. Flip. 5 min.

**Watch**
- High heat and the bottom burns before the middle sets.
- The three pitfalls people name are not in the text. Watch the heat.
- Plantain and banana are both written. They will not taste the same.

## 69. Beef and Onion Stir-Fry
Cook! Stacey Cook · time unknown · serves 4
https://youtu.be/wJ_vNUSQMZg

**Remember**
- Slice the beef against the grain.
- Mix the seasonings, then add the oil and sesame oil.
- Onions stay crunchy. The cornstarch is already on the beef. Finish with plain water, not another slurry.

**Do**
1. 190 g onion in strips. 4 scallions, whites and greens apart, 2-inch pieces. ¼ tsp salt and ¼ tsp sugar for the onion. 4 garlic cloves.
2. 480 g beef. Marinade: 2 tsp soy, 2¼ tsp dark soy, ¾ tsp oyster, ¾ tsp sugar, 1½ tsp baking soda, 4½ tsp cornstarch. Then 3 tsp oil and 1½ tsp sesame oil.
3. Hot pan. Onion and scallion whites, brief. Out.
4. 3 tbsp oil. Beef in a single layer. Sear, then flip. Garlic. At about 70% , 1½ tbsp cooking wine at the edge of the pan. 3 tsp dark soy, 1½ tsp oyster, ⅓ cup water. Pepper. Onions back.

**Watch**
- Oil into the meat before the soy is mixed through.
- Onions cooked until soft. She wants crunch.
- A second starch slurry. She already starched the beef.

**Tools** (hide)
- Knife, pan.

Taste 8 · Nutrition 6 · Unique 5
16.9 million views. The velvet beef. Water at the end, not more cornstarch.

Legume: soy sauce and dark soy. Oyster sauce often has soy. No peanuts.

## 70. Beef and Broccoli
Derek Chen · about 30 min · servings unknown
https://youtu.be/BKutdsvJqEo

**Remember**
- Flank against the grain.
- Leave the beef alone for 1–2 minutes so it sears.
- Broccoli gets 45 seconds in salted water. That is all.

**Do**
1. 1 lb flank. ¼ tsp baking soda, 3 tbsp Shaoxing, 1 tsp light soy, 1 tsp oyster, ½ tsp salt, ½ tsp sugar, 1 tbsp cornstarch, 1 tbsp oil.
2. Sauce: ⅓ cup beef broth, 1½ tbsp light soy, 1 tsp dark soy, 1 tbsp oyster, 1 tsp sesame oil, 1 tbsp sugar, ¼ tsp white pepper.
3. Blanch 1 head broccoli 45 seconds.
4. Ripping-hot wok, 3 tbsp oil. Beef in one layer. Wait, then stir. 3–4 min total. Out.
5. More oil, 3 garlic and 1 tsp ginger, a few seconds. 2 tbsp Shaoxing. Beef, broccoli, sauce. Slurry: 1½ tbsp cornstarch and 3 tbsp water. Sesame seeds. Rice.

**Watch**
- Stir the beef the second it hits and you get no crust.
- Broccoli left in the boil and it is olive drab.
- The oil total on the list is 5 tbsp. The steps use 3, then more. Don’t drown it.

**Tools** (hide)
- Wok.

Taste 8 · Nutrition 6 · Unique 4
893k views. The weeknight beef and broccoli with a real sear.

Legume: light soy and dark soy. No peanuts.

## 71. Movie Theater Popcorn
Half-Ass Movie Podcast · time unknown · one big bowl
https://youtu.be/w3wgQ0m6y_o

Partial. The method is spoken. Almost nothing is weighed. He does not want peanut oil.

**Remember**
- Heat below medium. Not high.
- Four kernels first. When those pop, add the rest, just enough to cover the bottom.
- A bowl waiting, and a glass lid so you can see.

**Do**
1. Coconut oil to cover the bottom of a heavy pot. Melt it.
2. Four test kernels. When they pop, kernels in a single loose layer. Yellow popcorn. Orville is fine.
3. Flavacol to taste. He says it should taste like salt, not a salt lick. Swirl.
4. Shake once it starts. Dump when the pops thin out.
5. A dash of salt, and a colored topping oil if you want the movie look. Soy in that oil is unknown. Skip it if you are avoiding soy.

**Watch**
- High heat and it scorches.
- A heavy hand of Flavacol.
- An opaque lid and you miss the moment.

**Tools** (hide)
- Heavy pot, glass lid, big bowl.

Taste 7 · Nutrition 2 · Unique 5
About 2.1 million views. Home popcorn that smells like a lobby.

No peanuts. He rejects peanut oil. Topping oil may be soy. Unknown.

## 72. Copycat Perfect Protein Bars
Colleen Forton · about 7 min plus 1 hr chill · about 6 bars a flavor
https://youtu.be/o-dIyREwowA
https://colleenshealthylife.com/copycat-perfect-protein-bars/

**Remember**
- Peanut butter is in the chocolate-peanut bars and in the caramel drizzle. Skip both.
- The brownie uses chocolate hazelnut spread. The caramel dough uses cashew butter. Those two doughs have no peanut.
- If the nut butter is not drippy, leave out the coconut flour. Chill 1 hour before you slice.

**Do**
1. Brownie, no peanut: ½ cup chocolate hazelnut spread, 6 tbsp chocolate protein, 3 tbsp almond flour, 1 tbsp cacao, 3 tbsp date syrup, ⅓ cup mini chips.
2. Caramel dough, no peanut: ½ cup cashew butter, 6 tbsp vanilla protein, 3 tbsp almond flour, 1 tbsp coconut flour only if the butter is drippy, 3 tbsp honey, 1 tsp vanilla, 3 medjool dates chopped in.
3. Press in a parchment loaf tin. Fridge 1 hour. Slice.
4. Do not make her drizzle. It is 2–3 tbsp peanut butter with coconut oil and maple.

**Watch**
- The peanut-butter flavor. It is half a cup of peanut butter.
- Runny nut butter and the bars will not hold. Add 1–2 tbsp coconut flour, not a cup of it. All coconut flour dries them out.
- Slice warm and they smear.

**Tools** (hide)
- Loaf tin, parchment.

Taste 7 · Nutrition 6 · Unique 4
1.1 million views. Candy-bar texture. Two flavors are safe. One is not.

Peanut: required in the peanut-butter bars and the drizzle. Skip those. Hazelnut and cashew doughs are the ones to make. No soy. No beans or lentils.

## 73. The Only Tuna Salad
Chef Jean-Pierre · 30 min · serves 6
https://youtu.be/7yl4K979AHM
https://chefjeanpierre.com/salad-recipes/the-only-way-to-make-tuna-salad/

**Remember**
- Drain the tuna hard. Solid, in water.
- Do not dump in all the dressing. Add until it holds.
- It is packed into hollow tomatoes. Do not freeze it.

**Do**
1. Two 12 oz cans solid tuna in water, drained, broken up.
2. Dressing: 1½ cups mayo, 1 tbsp Dijon, 2 tbsp yogurt, 2 tbsp sour cream.
3. Fold in 2 tbsp capers, 1 small shallot, 1 garlic, 1 tbsp dill, 1 tsp hot sauce, salt, pepper.
4. Then the tuna, then 1 celery stalk, ½ small bell pepper, ½ small fennel if you want it, a handful of chopped almonds.
5. More dressing only if it needs it. 6 hollow tomatoes. Pack. Serve cold.

**Watch**
- Wet tuna and the salad weeps.
- All 1½ cups of dressing and it is soup.
- The freezer. Mayo splits. Chickpeas are a comment suggestion, not this recipe. Don’t add them.

**Tools** (hide)
- Knives, spatula, bowl.

Taste 7 · Nutrition 6 · Unique 4
893k views. Tuna in a tomato. Almonds, not peanuts.

No peanuts. Almonds are in it. No legumes in the recipe.

## 74. New York Deli Tuna Salad

https://youtu.be/4iH4cuFlaRk

**Do**
1. Four 5 oz cans solid tuna in water, drained well.
2. ½ cup onion, ½ cup celery, 1 tsp celery salt, 2 tbsp sweet relish, 1 tbsp soy sauce. Sit 10 minutes.
3. 1¼ cups mayo, more later if it needs it. Salt and pepper.
4. Cover. Fridge overnight. Next day, drain the water. Add mayo only if it is dry.
5. Melt, if you want it: rye, tomato, American or Swiss.

**Watch**

- Cans that still hold water.
- Duke’s or Miracle Whip if you are chasing his deli taste. He says they are the wrong mayo.

2. Sauce: 32 oz jar marinara (she uses Rao’s), 28 oz crushed tomatoes, heavy cream, basil, Parmesan, ~2 tbsp sugar. Simmer.
3. Toss 1½ boxes ziti with 15 oz ricotta, ~1 cup mozzarella, same seasonings. Fold in some sauce.
4. Layer sauce, pasta, mozzarella in a pan. Top with mozzarella and Parmesan. Cover loosely with foil.
5. Bake 350°F about 30–40 minutes.

**Watch**

- Tight foil that steams the cheese into rubber.
- Undercooking the sausage before it goes in the sauce.

## 82. Moist & Fudgy Chocolate Cake
Whisk Takers · ~1 hr · two 9-inch layers
https://youtu.be/EaljSnLrJW8

**Remember**
- Boiling water + coffee thins the batter on purpose.
- 350°F / 175°C for 30 minutes. Cool 10 min in pans.
- Frost right away so it spreads. Remelt if it sets.

**Do**
1. Dry: 2 cups (400 g) sugar, 1¾ cups (220 g) flour, 1 cup (85 g) cocoa, heaping tsp (5 g) baking powder, heaping tsp (7 g) baking soda, ⅓ tsp (2 g) salt.
2. Wet into well: 2 eggs, 1 cup (240 ml) milk, ½ cup (120 ml) oil, 2 tsp vanilla, 1 tsp (3 g) coffee in 1 cup (240 ml) boiling water. Mix smooth.
3. Divide into greased, parchment-lined 9-inch pans. Bake 350°F 30 min. Cool 10 min.
4. Frosting: cook 3 yolks + 1 heaping tbsp (20 g) sugar + ⅔ cup (140 ml) cream with 200 g chocolate; whip with another ⅔ cup cream + 1 heaping tbsp sugar. Assemble immediately.

**Watch**
- Skipping parchment — layers stick.
- Frosting that hardens before you spread — double-boiler remelt, then whip.
- Cold eggs/milk if you can wait; room temp blends cleaner.

**Tools** (hide)
- Bowl, Whisk, Baking pan / tin, Parchment, Oven.

## 91. Beef and Broccoli Under 30 Minutes
Takes Two Eggs · ~15–30 min · serves 3
https://youtu.be/frJ00IEnAz0
https://takestwoeggs.com/beef-and-broccoli/

**Remember**
- Velvet the flank with baking soda and cornstarch slurry.
- Blanch broccoli 1–2 min for snap.
- Hard sear 30 sec/side, sauce last.

**Do**
1. Marinate 1 lb thinly sliced flank 15 min: 1 tsp baking soda, 1 tbsp Shaoxing, 1 tbsp light soy, 1 tbsp oyster sauce, ¼ tsp white pepper, 2 tbsp water, 1 tbsp cornstarch.
2. Sauce: 1½ tbsp oyster, 2 tbsp light soy, 2 tbsp dark soy, 1 tbsp Shaoxing, 1 tbsp sugar, ½ cup low-sodium chicken broth, ½ tbsp cornstarch.
3. Blanch 1 lb broccoli 1–2 min. Sear beef in hot wok ~30 sec/side. Remove.
4. Garlic (5 cloves) + ½ tbsp ginger 30 sec. Broccoli 1 min. Beef + sauce 1–2 min. Off heat, 1 tsp sesame oil.

**Watch**
- Crowded sear steams the beef.
- Oyster sauce and soy are named legumes/soy risks.
- Over-blanched broccoli turns army green.

## 101. Jam-Filled Muffins

https://youtu.be/w_aynUanIqU

**Remember**
- Dry and wet bowls separate. Mix until flour just disappears.
- Batter stays thick. Jam goes on top, not stirred in.
- 400°F about 22 min. Jam is hot when they come out.

**Do**
1. Mix 1 3/4 cups flour, 1/4 cup sugar, 2 1/2 tsp baking powder, 3/4 tsp salt.
2. Whisk 1 egg, 3/4 cup milk, 1/3 cup oil. Pour into dry. Stir barely.
3. Greased muffin pan, 1/2–2/3 full. Spoon jam on each. Bake 400°F ~22 min.
4. Cool a few minutes in the pan, then rack.

**Watch**
- Overmixing toughens the crumb.
- Jam burns if the spoonful sits on the rim.
- Pulling them too soon leaves wet centers under the jam.

## 124. Egg Muffins - 4 Easy Breakfast Meal Preps
Toasty Apron · oven 20 min @ 350°F / air fryer 12–15 @ 300°F · 6 muffins per batch
https://youtu.be/Ev9QcU-EmGc

**Remember**
- Base rule: 6 large eggs per 6-cup tin. Oil-spray the pan.
- Three methods: ready-to-use mix-ins, sautéed mix-ins, cottage-cheese blend sous-vide style.
- No air fryer for the water-bath method.

**Do**
1. Method 1 meat/veggie: whisk 6 eggs + 1/4 tsp each garlic powder, onion powder, salt, pepper. Spray tin. Pour. Fill with 2 tbsp each spinach/onion/ham/cheddar (+ chili flake) or spinach/pepper/scallion/tomato/mozzarella (+ parsley). Bake.
2. Method 2 Tex Mex: sauté 1/2 lb pork sausage, 1/4 cup each red/green pepper, 1/4 cup onion, 1/4 cup corn, 1/2 tbsp taco seasoning. Whisk 4 eggs + 1/2 cup egg whites + 1/2 tbsp taco seasoning. Fill + 1/4 cup Tex Mex cheese. Bake.
3. Method 3: blend 4 eggs + 1/2 cup cottage cheese + 1/2 tsp Tabasco + 1/2 tsp garlic powder. Fill with onion, turkey, steamed broccoli, feta, oregano. Optional water bath. Oven only.
4. Oven 20 min 350°F or air fryer 12–15 min 300°F. Cool 5 min. Fridge 3–4 days / freeze 2–3 months.

**Watch**
- Taco seasoning can hide soy.
- Air-frying the water-bath tray is unsafe/not advised.
- Skipping oil spray sticks the muffins hard.

**Tools** (hide)

## 125. 15-minute Staple Chinese Food: Tomato Egg Stir Fry
Ms Shi and Mr He · 15 min · serves 2

**Remember**
- Eggs first, 30 seconds, still wet in the middle.
- 4 tomatoes and 4 eggs. About 15 minutes, serves 2.
- Light soy is optional, and it is still soy.

**Do**
1. X the tomatoes, boil 30 seconds, ice bath, peel, then wedge. Mince 1 garlic clove and slice 1 scallion.
2. Whisk 4-5 eggs with 1/2 tsp salt. Stir-fry in 3 tbsp very hot oil for 30 seconds and pull them while still wet.
3. Medium-high, 2 tbsp oil, garlic and scallion whites about 10 seconds. Add tomatoes, 1/2 tsp salt, 1 tbsp light soy, 2 tbsp ketchup, and 1/2 tbsp sugar.
4. If the pan is dry, add 1/4 cup water or stock. Boil about 1 minute, return the eggs for 30 seconds, and finish with scallion greens.

**Watch**
- Ingredient list says 3 tbsp oil for the eggs; one step says 2 tbsp.
- Salt with the tomatoes is 1/2 tsp on the list and 1/2 tbsp in one step. Follow the list.
- Soy sauce and ketchup are optional. Skipping soy means add more salt.

4. Pinch of salt, 2 tbsp vegan butter, rosemary, chives, and pepper.

**Watch**
- Add broth only until the pasta is done and the sauce is still a thick slurry.
- He starts checking at 5 minutes and covers for the last 5.
- Shallots want medium-low. Garlic goes in after, not with the long simmer.

3. Gyudon: soy sauce, mirin, all-in-one seasoning instead of dashi, and sugar. Oil, sliced onion, then thin sukiyaki beef until the pink is gone. Eggs cook in the residual heat. Serve with rice, sesame seeds, and scallions.
4. Mango sticky rice: cook washed sticky rice like normal rice. Heat 2 cans coconut milk with sugar and salt. Sweeten some of the rice with part of it. Thicken the rest with a cornstarch slurry, microwave in 30-second bursts. Top with mango and toasted sesame.

**Watch**
- The cooker can drop to warm. She wants high heat to brown the mushrooms.
- Fish sauce, soy, mirin, and sugar are not given as amounts.

## 130. my go-to one pot lazy dish
Nomadette Eats · unknown · unknown
https://youtu.be/K9VvLH-tKFE

Marinate 250g bite-size chicken thigh with 1 tsp garlic paste, 1 tsp ginger paste, 2 tbsp light soy, 2 tsp sugar, 2 tsp apple cider vinegar, 1 tsp sesame oil, 1/4 tsp black pepper, and optional 1/2 tsp dashi or chicken-stock powder. Top with 1 sliced onion. 10 minutes, or overnight. The intro also says about 20.

3. Lightly stir 2 eggs, not fully beaten. When the rice finishes, pour them on, lid on 2-5 minutes.
4. Scallions. Stir gently so the seasoning spreads. Optional donburi sauce: 1 cup water, 1/4 cup soy sauce, 2 tbsp sugar, 1 tbsp apple cider vinegar, boiled until the sugar dissolves.

**Watch**
- Light soy is required. Snow peas are only an optional add-in.
- Page says marinate 10 minutes in the steps and about 20 in the intro.
- Five minutes of egg steam sets them solid. She wants a jiggle closer to 2.

**Tools** (hide)

- Arrowroot or tapioca can turn gummy in this long simmer. She switched to cornstarch.

## 135. Never Buy Teriyaki Sauce Again!
LifebyMikeG · about 15 min · unknown
https://youtu.be/rZaZhD_MlaI
https://lifebymikeg.com/blogs/all/why-every-cook-should-master-chicken-teriyaki

**Remember**
- Equal parts sake, mirin, and soy sauce.
- His written batch is 1 cup of each.
- Cornstarch slurry at the end, then sesame seeds.

**Do**
1. Fine-chop 2 garlic cloves and a knob of ginger. Soften in oil on low. The short says about 2 minutes; the page says about 3, and lists 2 tbsp oil while one step says 1 tbsp.
2. Add 1 cup sake, 1 cup mirin, and 1 cup soy sauce. Simmer about 5 minutes, until reduced by about a third.
3. Stir in 2 tbsp cornstarch mixed with 4 tbsp water until thick.
4. Off heat, about 1 tbsp sesame seeds. Cool, then jar it.

## 141. Most DELICIOUS Protein Brownies You'll Ever Try!
VADE Nutrition · 25-30 min bake · unknown
https://youtu.be/fi9bEEnKwsk

- They say the batch has 100g of protein.

**Do**
1. Beat 1 stick softened butter with 1 cup sugar. Mix in 2 eggs.
2. Add 5 scoops VADE chocolate protein and 1/2 cup of the 3/4 cup milk until the powder dissolves.
3. Add 1 cup flour, 1/2 cup cocoa, 1 tsp vanilla, the rest of the milk, and optional salt and 1 tsp espresso. Not the chips yet.
4. Fold in 1/2 cup chocolate chips. Pan, press on the other 1/2 cup.
5. 350F, 25-30 minutes. An 11x7 or 13x9 cooks more evenly. An 8x8 stays gooey in the middle.

**Watch**
- An 8x8 will look underdone in the center on purpose.
- Salt and espresso are optional, not in the base mix.

## 143. EASIEST Carnivore Pancakes EVER (Dairy Free)

https://youtu.be/zVUHEV3GRdY
https://steak-and-butter-gal.myklpages.com/l/WuXdQW

**Remember**
- Three ingredients: eggs, collagen, and gelatin. No dairy in the batter.
- 3 eggs, 1 tbsp gelatin, and 2 1/2 tbsp collagen make 3 pancakes.
- Lid on 8 minutes, flip, lid on 5 more.

**Do**
1. Separate yolks and whites. For 3 eggs, beat the whites on low while streaming in 1 tbsp gelatin to stiff peaks. Six eggs use 2 tbsp.
2. Beat the yolks, then 2 1/2 tbsp collagen per 3 eggs (two packets, 5 tbsp, when she doubles). Fold the whites in. Clumps are fine.
3. Preheat the pan about 5 minutes on high until water droplets dance. Grease with tallow or lard. Drop to low-medium.
4. About 1/4 cup batter, shaped into a round. Lid on 8 minutes, flip, lid on 5 minutes.

**Watch**
- Too hot and they deflate and stain dark. She stays at low-medium after the preheat.
- 2 tbsp gelatin is the doubled, 6-egg batch, not the 3-egg one.
- She wants the pan fully preheated or the batter sticks.

**Tools** (hide)
- Skillet / pan, Lid, Bowl, Whisk.

## 145. How to Sharpen a Knife with a Whetstone
**SET ASIDE — not a recipe.** Knife sharpening technique, not cooking

TheRenderQ · about 5 min soak plus sharpening · unknown
https://youtu.be/h6faatE8b9Q

Thin card.

**Remember**
- This is not a recipe.
- It is a whetstone sharpening demo.
- The stone is 1000 grit, then 6000.

**Do**
1. Soak the stone until the bubbles slow, about 5 minutes.
2. On 1000 grit, hold about 15 degrees and make about 12-15 light strokes a side.
3. Flip to 6000 grit for about 10-12 passes a side, then test on paper.

**Watch**
- He says light pressure. Pushing hard is not the method.
- Changing the angle mid-stroke rolls the edge.
- This stone wants water, not oil.

Taste — · Nutrition — · Unique —
A how-to with about 1.9 million views. People are there for the paper test, not food.

No peanuts. No legumes.

4. Simmer 2½–3 hr. Thicken with 2 Tbsp masa harina or flour if needed. Finish with 1 Tbsp OG + 1 Tbsp WOW to taste.
5. Condiments at his house: onion, cheese, salsa verde, chili piquin, habanero; beans optional.

**Watch**

5. Warm corn tortillas; top with pork, pineapple, onion, cilantro, lime.

1. Marinate 800 g cubed chicken breast with 4 minced garlic, 20–30 g tomato paste, 1 tsp salt & pepper, 2 tsp onion powder, 1 tsp cinnamon, 2 tsp paprika, 2 tsp olive oil, lemon juice. Overnight preferred.

3. Cook chicken: oven 15 min or air fry 12 min at 190°C / 375°F.
4. Toss 500 g cooked basmati with salt, paprika, turmeric. Side salad: tomato, red onion, parsley, salt, pepper, garlic powder, lemon.
5. Bowl: golden rice, chicken, sauce, salad.

**Watch**
- Cashews are tree nuts, not peanuts.
- Skipping the blend and the sauce stays chunky.
- Overbaking the lean breast dry.

**Tools** (hide)

## 175. Mango Muffins
RecipeTin Eats · about 24 min bake · serves 12
https://youtu.be/rzNUxT_Dayg
https://www.recipetineats.com/mango-muffins/

**Remember**
- Two big mangoes: puree in batter + chopped mango stirred in.
- Start hot then drop oven temp for tall domes.
- Desiccated coconut + demerara crunch on top.

**Do**
1. Preheat 220°C/425°F (200°C fan). Grease muffin tin (no liners).
2. Whisk dry: 2¼ cups flour, 1¼ tsp baking soda, pinch salt. Whisk wet: ⅓ cup melted unrefined coconut oil (or butter), 1 egg, 1 tsp white vinegar, ¾ cup sugar, 1 tsp vanilla, 1 cup (260 g) mango puree.
3. Fold dry into wet almost combined; stir in 2 cups (370 g) small-chopped mango + ½ cup unsweetened desiccated coconut. Fill holes; sprinkle ~½ tsp demerara each.
4. Put in oven; immediately drop to 200°C/390°F (180°C fan). Bake 24 min until golden and skewer clean. Cool 10 min in pan, 10 on rack.

**Watch**
- Too much puree turns pudding-wet; too little dries out.
- Cold mango from fridge slows rise — use room temp.
- Liners peel too much muffin off.

**Tools** (hide)
- Oven, Muffin pan, Bowl, Whisk, Cooling rack (optional).

Taste 8 · Nutrition 4 · Unique 6

No peanuts. No legumes.

## 187. Feeding Napoleon - Chicken Marengo
Tasting History with Max Miller · under 1 hour cook · serves unknown
https://youtu.be/O1flfls4N78
https://www.tastinghistory.com/recipes/chickenmarengo

**Remember**

- Roux from the pan oil + flour, then beef broth sauce.
- Optional mushrooms; strain the sauce.

**Do**
1. Heat 100 ml olive oil medium-high. Add chicken (Max: 4 drumsticks + 4 thighs) without overlapping; salt ½ tsp, pepper ¼ tsp. Add 2 whole peeled shallots, 1 garlic clove, bay leaf, thyme sprig, parsley bunch. Cover: fry 10 min, flip 10 min, flip 5 min more.
2. Remove chicken; keep warm. Pull large herbs/shallots aside. Stir 40 g flour into hot oil for a roux a couple minutes; return herbs/shallots; cook until golden.
3. Add 500 ml beef broth; scrape fond; simmer stirring 10 min. Strain sauce. Optional: sauté mushrooms. Plate chicken, pour sauce, garnish mushrooms.

**Watch**
- Overlapping pieces steam instead of brown.
- Split sauce — splash water and stir low heat.
- Modern tomato-crayfish Marengo is a different dish.

3. Fold in onions. Refrigerate ≥2 hours. Serve room temp with crinkle-cut chips; chives optional.

- Rushing onions browns bitter instead of sweet.

## 190. The Cheesiest Homemade Texas Queso (2 Ways)

https://www.joshuaweissman.com/post/homemade-texas-queso

**Remember**
- Two queso paths: American-cheese traditional vs cheddar-gruyere fancy.
- No roux needed for the American version — cheese emulsifies milk.
- Fancy version starts with butter-shallot-serrano roux.

**Do**
1. Traditional: heat 2 tbsp neutral oil; sweat diced onion + 2 diced jalapeños with salt; add 3 chopped garlic cloves; stir in 1 diced tomato until hot. Melt in ¾ lb grated American + ¼ lb pepper jack with 1 cup whole milk, stirring to silky. Finish ¼ cup cilantro; garnish tomato + cilantro.
2. Fancy: melt 2 tbsp butter; soften 2 diced shallots, 4 sliced serranos, 3 garlic cloves. Sprinkle 2 tbsp flour 30 sec; whisk 1 cup milk until thick. Off heat, whisk in 4½ cups cheddar + 1 cup gruyere in batches, then ½ cup sour cream.
3. Serve fancy over salted diced avocado; garnish pickled red onion, serrano, cilantro, hot sauce.

**Watch**
- Boiling American cheese sauce can go grainy — medium heat, constant stir.
- Hot sauce garnish may hide soy — not required in the base.
- Fancy recipe serves more cheese than the ‘serves 2’ tag suggests — scale bowls.

## 193. Easy Beef Stew - How to Make The Easiest Way
Chowhound · about 2 hr 45 min · serves 4–6

**Remember**
- Brown floured chuck first; braise before veg.
- Add carrots, celery, potatoes after the meat hour.
- Frozen peas at the end for color.

**Do**
1. Toss 3 lb cubed chuck in ¼ cup flour + 2 tsp kosher salt + 1 tsp pepper. Brown in 3 tbsp oil in 3 batches; set aside.
2. Soften diced onion; cook 2 tbsp tomato paste 1–2 min; sprinkle remaining 1 tbsp flour 1 min. Add 1 cup red wine; thicken ~3 min. Return beef; add 4 cups low-sodium beef broth, 2 bay leaves, 4 thyme sprigs. Simmer uncovered 1 hour.
3. Add large-dice carrots, celery, Yukon Gold potatoes; cover and simmer ~1 hour more until tender. Discard bay/thyme stems; stir in 1 cup frozen peas ~5 min. Season.

**Watch**
- Crowding the sear steams grey meat.
- Peas are legumes.
- Adding veg too early turns them to mush.

## 204. The Secrets to Easy & Delicious Pan Sauces | Techniquely with Lan Lam
America's Test Kitchen · about 11 min video · serves unknown
https://youtu.be/z8Y88GS7TSg
https://cooks.io/3CEyn8j
https://cooks.io/3XlkANt

**Remember**

- Demo: crispy-skin chicken with vinegar-pepper sauce.
- 1 tsp flour in the fat makes a quick gravy that won’t break.

**Do**
1. Bone/skin prep: remove ribcage/wishbone/tender; pound; cold pan with ~2 tbsp oil, skin-down under weight; medium 7–9 min, then 6–8 min; flip to 160°F ~2–3 min; rest.
2. Sauce: leave ~2 tsp fat; soften minced shallot; sprinkle 1 tsp flour, cook without darkening.
3. Add chicken broth + pickled pepper brine; simmer 2–3 min to a scant 1/2 cup.
4. Off heat: resting juices, chopped cherry peppers, minced thyme, 1 tbsp cold butter. Sauce around, not on, the crisp skin.

**Watch**
- Linked full recipes sit behind cooks.io login.
- Browning the flour past fond-color makes the sauce taste toasted.
- Hot butter dumps instead of cold and the emulsion can break.

**Tools** (hide)
- Skillet / Pan, Knife, Cutting board, Whisk, Thermometer.

## 205. Chicken Kiev - Crispy Chicken Breast Stuffed with Garlic Butter Sauce - How to Make Chicken Kiev

https://youtu.be/79wknoNrGz0

**Remember**
- Garlic-parsley butter pocket, freeze firm, then bread.
- Deep-fry briefly, then 400°F oven — don’t cut to check.

**Do**
1. Butter: crush 2 garlic cloves with 6 tbsp unsalted butter, 2 tbsp parsley, pinch salt; chill.
2. Pound 4×8-oz breasts; wrap ~1½ tbsp butter each; freeze firm.
3. Dredge: salted flour (1 cup + 2 tsp salt), 2 beaten eggs, 2 cups panko.
4. Fry in ~2 inches oil 2–3 min; bake 400°F about 15–17 min until cooked; rest 5 min.

**Watch**
- Undercooked center if breasts are bigger than 8 oz — add a few minutes.
- Poking to check doneness dumps the butter early.
- Crowding the oven tray and they need more time.

**Remember**
- 100% agave tequila, fresh lime, Cointreau — skip the mix.
- Big ice for rocks; frozen needs different ratios.
- Salt the rim first.

**Do**
1. Rocks: salt rim. Shake 2 oz tequila + 1 oz Cointreau + 3/4 oz fresh lime; pour over large ice.
2. Frozen (each drink): 2 oz tequila, 1 oz Cointreau, 1 oz simple syrup, 1 1/4 oz lime; for two drinks blend with 2 1/2 cups ice; salt rim.
3. Avoid mixto tequila, bottled lime juice, tiny ice, and margarita mix.

**Watch**
- Blending the rocks ratio with ice makes a watery slush.
- Mixto tequila (grain spirits) tastes harsh.
- Packaged lime juice uses lime oil — not the same.

**Tools** (hide)
- Jar (optional), Blender (optional), Pitcher (optional).

## 223. Chocolate Coconut Macaroons Recipe Demonstration - Joyofbaking.com
Joy of Baking · chill ~1 hr; bake 13–15 min at 325°F · makes about 24
https://youtu.be/SBzQuBckTj8
http://www.joyofbaking.com/ChocolateCoconutMacaroons.html

**Remember**
- Melt chocolate; whisk whites with cocoa/sugar.
- Fold coconut; chill until scoopable.
- 325°F until shiny and just set.

**Do**
1. Melt 4 oz chopped chocolate over simmering water; cool to room temp.
2. Whisk 3 egg whites; add 1/4 cup sifted cocoa, 3/4 cup sugar, 1/4 tsp salt, 1 tsp vanilla; stir in 2 1/2 cups sweetened coconut + melted chocolate. Chill ~1 hr.
3. Scoop heaping tablespoons onto parchment; bake 325°F (165°C) 13–15 min until shiny/set. Cool 10 min on sheet, then rack.

**Watch**
- Undissolved cocoa lumps if you skip sifting.
- Skipping the chill and the mounds spread.
- Overbaking dries the chewy candy-bar center.

**Tools** (hide)
- Saucepan, Bowl, Whisk, Oven, Baking sheet, Parchment, Wire rack.

2. Medium-high: ginger/garlic in 2 Tbsp oil ~10 sec; high heat chicken ~2 min; chilies 1 min.
3. Medium: sauce 2 min; slurry 1 min until cooked (~5–6 min total chicken time).
4. Scallion garnish; rice or noodles.

**Watch**
- Soy required — soy/legume.
- Oyster sauce can hide soy; hoisin sub is also soy/legume.
- Fresh chilies make it very spicy — start low.

**Tools** (hide)
- Skillet / Pan, Bowl.

Taste 8 · Nutrition 6 · Unique 5
55.8k views. Quick chili chicken.

No peanuts. Legumes: soy sauce required; hoisin optional.

## 272. Creamy Garlic Chicken Breast Recipe
Break the Spice · about 4 min video · serves 2
https://youtu.be/8Er5fjgOhhc

**Remember**
- One-pan creamy garlic chicken breasts.
- Lime stands in for wine acidity.
- Low flame after cream so it doesn’t split.

**Do**
1. Season and sear 2 chicken breasts in olive oil/butter; set aside.
2. Cook onion + garlic; add 1/2 cup stock (or water + cube); reduce; 1 tsp lime.
3. Low heat: 1/2 cup cream, oregano, parsley; salt/pepper late.
4. Return chicken to coat; optional parmesan. Serve with pasta/rice/veg.

**Watch**
- High heat after cream can split the sauce.
- Stock cubes are salty — salt at the end.
- No soy/peanut in the printed list.

**Tools** (hide)
- Skillet / Pan.

Taste 8 · Nutrition 5 · Unique 3
10 million views. Huge creamy-garlic hit.

No peanuts. No legumes.
